+              ".zip";
+            this.peService.createAndDownloadBlobFile(response, fileName);
+            this.setAlertVisibility(
+              true,
+              AlertType.SUCCESS,
+              "Exhibits Zip Downloaded Successfully."
+            );
+          } else {
+            this.setAlertVisibility(
+              true,
+              AlertType.ERROR,
+              "Exhibits Zip Download Failed."
+            );
+          }
+        },
+        (errorResponse) => {
+          if (
+            errorResponse &&
+            errorResponse.error &&
+            errorResponse.error instanceof Blob
+          ) {
+            const reader: FileReader = new FileReader();
+            reader.readAsText(errorResponse.error);
+            reader.onload = (e) => {
+              var errMsg = JSON.parse(
+                JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(e.target.result))
+              );
+              if (
+                errMsg &&
+                errMsg.exceptions &&
+                errMsg.exceptions.exceptions[0] &&
+                errMsg.exceptions.exceptions[0].type ==
+                "java.io.FileNotFoundException"
+              ) {
+                this.showExhibitsZipDownloadLink = false;
+                this.setAlertVisibility(
+                  true,
+                  AlertType.WARNING,
+                  "The bulk download exhibit link has expired. Please re-create, if desired.",
+                  false
+                );
+                return;
+              }
+              if (isOnloadFlow) {
+                this.showExhibitsZipDownloadLink = true;
+                return;
+              }
+            };
+          } else if (isOnloadFlow) {
+            this.showExhibitsZipDownloadLink = true;
+            return;
+          }
+          if (!isOnloadFlow) {
+            this.setAlertVisibility(
+              true,
+              AlertType.ERROR,
+              "Exhibits Zip Download Failed."
+            );
+          }
+        });
   }
